Source: live frontier scan, captured_at 2026-08-02T23:40:42Z, 8691 buckets, 1,553,347 nodes processed, elapsed 374.4s (calm load, no throttling — was 45min/hard-capped in every prior attempt tonight).

df used 807.9 GiB = measured 524.2 GiB (64.9%) + residual 277.2 GiB (34.3%).

**Mail/Messages/MobileSync now directly measured** (previously blocked by the persistent EINTR, resolved by tonight's reboot): Mail 3.40 GiB, Messages 1.04 GiB, MobileSync ~0 GiB (empty). Combined 4.4 GiB — confirms the earlier manual `du` cross-check exactly and falsifies the ~75-165 GiB estimate this investigation had been chasing.

**Honest nuance**: despite ~15x more buckets than this morning's run (8691 vs 499) and far more than the previous best full run (12,076 on 07-29), overall attribution % (64.9%) is roughly similar-to-slightly-lower than 07-29's 68.6% — the scan log shows a real (non-EINTR) `inventory_permission_denied` on at least one ancestor this run, excluding a chunk of otherwise-real data from the accepted total. More buckets != strictly more coverage when a different gap opens elsewhere.
